{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"gnomes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An elemental (spirit or corporeal creature associated with a classical element) associated with earth."],"qualifier":"magic","topics":["alchemy","pseudoscience"]},{"glosses":["One of a race of imaginary human-like beings, usually depicted as short and typically bearded males, who inhabit the inner parts of the earth and act as guardians of mines, mineral treasure, etc.; in modern fantasy literature and games, when distinguished from dwarves, gnomes are usually smaller than dwarves and more focused on engineering than mining."],"topics":["fantasy","human-sciences","mysticism","mythology","philosophy","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A person of a small stature or misshapen features, or of a strange appearance."],"tags":["derogatory","informal"]},{"glosses":["The mountain pygmy owl, Glaucidium gnoma, a small owl of the western United States."]},{"glosses":["A small statue of a dwarf-like character, often bearded, placed in a garden."],"synonyms":[{"word":"garden gnome"},{"word":"lawn gnome"}]},{"glosses":["An upper atmospheric optical phenomenon associated with thunderstorms, a compact blue starter."],"topics":["astronomy","climatology","meteorology","natural-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the gnomes of Zurich","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A banker, especially a secretive international one."],"tags":["derogatory","often"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈnəʊ̯m/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈnoʊ̯m/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈnɔʊ̯m/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈnəʉ̯m/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":174625,"upstream_offset":824184529,"upstream_record_sha256":"e1b6a34645ecc69290bf7c62337d2c793a75bb8491c55237cfe6bc3d444ecefd","word":"gnome"}

{"forms":[{"form":"subpoenas","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"subpoenae","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"subpoenæ","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"subpena","tags":["alternative","dated"]},{"form":"subpœna","tags":["alternative","archaic"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A writ (“written order”) requiring someone to appear in court, or at a deposition or some other legal proceeding, as a witness to give testimony (a subpoena ad testificandum) or to produce evidence (subpoena duces tecum), in default of which the person may be punished."],"synonyms":[{"word":"witness summons"},{"word":"order to attend court"},{"word":"citation"},{"word":"invocation"},{"word":"subpoena"},{"word":"summons"},{"word":"writ of summons"}],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["A writ requiring a defendant to appear in court to answer a plaintiff's claim (a subpoena ad respondendum); in England and Wales, and Ireland, this writ was formerly issued by the Court of Chancery at the plaintiff's request to commence a suit, but the procedure was abolished in 1852."],"tags":["historical"],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["A motive or thing which can compel or demand something, or summon someone."],"tags":["figuratively"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/sə(b)ˈpiː.nə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/sə(b)ˈpi.nə/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":40165,"upstream_offset":287628520,"upstream_record_sha256":"8604b5b63bd20c243f13f74e3cfb707422d74d127e993142c374ce3832826350","word":"subpoena"}
{"forms":[{"form":"subpoenas","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"subpoenaing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"subpoenaed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"subpoenaed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"subpena","tags":["alternative","dated"]},{"form":"subpœna","tags":["alternative","archaic"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To summon (someone) with a subpoena (noun sense 1) to appear in court, or at a deposition or some other legal proceeding, as a witness to give testimony or to produce evidence; to serve a subpoena on (someone)."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["To require (a document or other evidence) to be produced before a court, or at a deposition or some other legal proceeding."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["To compel or summon (someone or something); to demand."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/sə(b)ˈpiː.nə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/sə(b)ˈpi.nə/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":40166,"upstream_offset":287657621,"upstream_record_sha256":"3b60d5f8671f6c57dd5ed9c2df09a171c4ce8bd868e13831c3cb521c4e65302e","word":"subpoena"}
